### Final Query

Identify every person who satisfies all of the following conditions:
c1. works for an organization based in a coastal city
c2. ordinarily travels using public transportation
c3. currently works a night shift

`matches` must be a JSON array containing every qualifying person-ID string.
Sort the person IDs lexicographically.
